| ⑨ | fix in place and re-run, the re-run counts into R (§9.2); **R≥2 ⇒ apply the §5.2 high-risk row** (⑤ re-check becomes required); **R>3 ⇒ pause and wait for human review**; fall back to ① only when an architecture assumption is falsified by native evidence |
| ⑧c | a wrap-up cleanup finding ⇒ fix in place and **re-run ⑧c** until all green (frozen evidence packs run the self-consistency check per the ⑧c exemption entry in §5.1); a failed pre-⑩ final-state readback (the "post-⑧c evidence window" clause of §5.1) ⇒ clean up in place and re-run that readback until it passes |

---

## 6. Gates and Evidence

### 6.1 Base Gates (after every change)

| Gate | Command / criterion |
|---|---|
| Format | `gofmt -l .` output is empty |
| Build | `go build ./...` exit 0 |
| Static check | `go vet ./...` exit 0 |
| Unit tests | `go test ./...` exit 0 (record the count of ok packages and the FAIL count) |
| Contract fixtures | when contracts are involved, additionally run the Node contract check (fixture counts must stay in sync) |
| Encoding | `.md`/`.ps1` = UTF-8 **with BOM** + LF; `.json`/`.go` etc. = **without BOM** + LF |

**Focused-gate definition (2026-09-23)**: `focused gate = go test ./[affected package]/... -run [test-point prefix]` (or the equivalent contract-fixture subset); the slice definition's "steps" field **must state that command**.

### 6.2 Evidence Persistence Obligation (Hard Rule R6.1)

**All review-type subagents lack the `execute` capability** (measured: `deep-reasoner`, `independent-reviewer` have only `read`+`search`). Therefore:

- Before review, the master must persist the **raw diff** and the **raw gate output** to disk (under the repo-root `tmp/`, cleaned up after use; the path rule is §1.5 / §1.5.1), and hand the **paths** to the reviewer.
- It is **forbidden** to paste only a diff summary/paraphrase into the prompt and treat that as "evidence provided".
- Precedent: the B3c ⑦ re-review was voided for an entire round because "the raw hunk was not provided"; after switching to persisting `tmp/b3c-review.diff` it passed in one round.

### 6.3 Closing Self-check Gate (nine mechanical checks, **mandatory**)

**Location**: `tools/gates/` (under version control). **Trigger**: before closing any slice, before any docs-only commit.
**Principle**: replace "human memory" with "mechanical assertions" — this directive does not assume that any role is incapable of forgetting.

| # | Check | Criterion |
|---|---|---|
| G1 | **Status-marker consistency** | The state symbol in a subsection heading, the status-table row and the completion-record row must agree (B3c once had a heading still `⬜` while the status table was already ✅). **Exemption (2026-09-23)**: a stopped-state slice (§1.6) does not take part in the three-place consistency judgement and follows the stopped-state marking rules of §1.6; G7-d covers its completeness. **v1.36 scripting definition (`GATES-G1G5`)**: the criterion judges the **anchorable** part only - (c) the state symbol belongs to the ledger's own closed set `⬜ / 🔄 / ✅ / ⛔` (`⏸` only on headings, for pending-evaluation items); (d) the same key carries the same symbol in the heading and the status table, and a status row whose key has no heading fails as a phantom row; (e) a completion-log key, once **bound**, requires its heading to be `✅` - binding is exact primary key first (`P11`), then a UNIQUE **backticked alias** in the heading name (`GATES-G1G5`); no binding is `INFO` with bound / unbound counts in the detail, **never a silent pass**; (f) an **added** completion row must not carry in-progress wording ("进行中" / "In progress" / awaiting finalisation) - historical rows are never re-judged (same scope rule as G1-a / G3 / G4), and self-referential rows containing `不得残留` / `示例` / `引文` / `冲突` / `矛盾` are skipped. A ledger without its state-symbol declaration line ⇒ exit 2 (the closed set is never guessed). **Recorded as unmechanised (stated honestly)**: (1) a `⬜` heading whose slice actually closed but has no completion row (needs knowledge from outside the slice); (2) a `✅` heading with no completion row (four instances in the current corpus, so the reverse implication ("`✅` ⇒ a completion row must exist") is **not satisfiable** today and is not judged); (3) the truthfulness of a summary; (4) the stopped-state (§1.6) exemption - all four rest on ⑤⑥⑦ plus human review. |
| G2 | **Placeholder residue** | the full text must not retain placeholders such as "待…回填" "尚未提交" "待同轮授权" "pending the push" (after the switchover these must already have been replaced by facts). **The scan scope must exclude this line's own rule quotation** — otherwise the forbidden strings quoted on this line would be judged by the line itself as violations (**self-reference defect**; measured 2026-09-21: §6.3 self-scan yielded 4 false positives); implement it as "forbidden string inside quotes + the line contains 『不得残留』 ⇒ skip" |
| G3 | **Bilingual symmetry** | (a) **Changed-line-count symmetry**: for every changed CN/EN document pair, the added/deleted line counts from `git diff --numstat` must be equal (measured 2026-09-21: all 6 pairs in this slice PASS). It is **not required** that full-text line counts or bold-marker counts be equal — existing bilingual pairs are historically asymmetric (`REMAINING_SLICES` 501/488, `B2-EXTERNAL-ENFORCEMENT` 270/315), and "full-text equality" is unsatisfiable for them (**self-reference defect**, same as G4b). (b) **Key-field alignment (scope = changed lines, not the full text)**: among the **added lines** of `git diff -U0`, the counts of **commit hash / run number / status enumeration / conclusion enumeration** on the CN and EN sides must be equal — line-count symmetry **cannot** prove that semantics did not drift (⑦ first-round Medium finding). **Do not** compare the full text: full-text differences may come from historical legacy (measured: `B2-EXTERNAL-ENFORCEMENT` full text 10/9, while changed lines 0/0). |
| G4 | **Encoding + anomalous characters** | (a) BOM+LF verified file by file: `.md` / `.ps1` carry exactly one BOM and every other type carries zero, counted over the consecutive prefix (OB-112, v1.44); **scope = the change set pending commit this time** (≠ the whole dirty tree). (b) **Anomalous-character scan**: the set of CJK characters in the added content ⊖ the HEAD repository-wide `.md` corpus = the **difference set**; the difference set then ⊖ `tools/gates/cjk-newwords.txt` (the legitimate-new-word allow-list, each character must come with a reason and a context excerpt) **must be empty**. ⇒ The original wording "the difference set must be empty" is unsatisfiable for any new document (measured 2026-09-21: a 51 KB new document had a 7-character difference set, all confirmed character by character as legitimate new words), so this is changed to an **allow-list** that fixes "human judgment" into a mechanically re-runnable assertion. (Origin of this rule: 「拒绝」 was once miswritten as 「拒绍」 in 5 places) **G4b cache (v1.49)**: the cross-process cache stores only the **corpus CJK character set** (a pure-function result) and **never the verdict**; the key must include the resolved sha, the whitelist text digest, the criterion source digest and the cache-format version prefix, and must be recomputed **before every read**; a changed `HEAD`, whitelist or criterion code ⇒ **guaranteed miss**; any cache anomaly must **fail open back to the full scan**, never changing the verdict or detail semantics. **Version-bump obligation**: if the enumeration or read semantics of `ctx.listAllMarkdown` / `ctx.showAt` / the git wrapper change (for example, a new suffix is included or the ref construction changes), the cache-format version prefix must be bumped in step; otherwise every old entry is treated as stale and must not be reused. |
| G5 | **Change-set contract** | Three things must agree: the **staged list**, the **change set declared by the report** and the **evidence references in the ledger**; `git add` must name files one by one (never `-A`). **v1.36 input model (`GATES-G1G5`)**: the four fields `{stagedFiles[], declaredFiles[], evidenceRefs[], addMode}` are **derived mechanically** by the criterion instead of demanding a JSON from outside the repository (that requirement amounted to self-attestation) - `stagedFiles` = the scope's change set **minus deleted paths** (a deletion declares nothing); `declaredFiles` = the `artifacts` fence rows of the changed `docs/validation/*.md` whose **disposition is `repo`**, plus **the report's own path** (`local-only` / `missing-historical` never enter the declared set); `evidenceRefs` = the paths in the evidence cell of **added** ledger completion rows - **both backticked and bare paths are recognised** (all 30 rows in the current corpus are bare, so backtick-only extraction was vacuous); `addMode` = a **derived label** (`explicit` / `incomplete` / `batch-suspect` / `unverifiable` / `n/a`) inferred from the observable consequences judged by (d) and (f), because **git does not record how a path was staged** (`-A` and per-file adds leave a byte-identical index) and therefore the label **never fails on its own**. Sub-checks: (d) **change set ⊆ declared set** - the reverse direction (declared but untouched) is `INFO`, because a fence is the snapshot of its landing commit (an 11.2 backfill commit measures 2 files against an 11-row fence, so a strict equality test would turn every backfill commit red in CI); `INFO` when no report is in the change set; (e) evidence references resolve (file or directory, scope-aware); (f) index scope only: **the worktree is clean** - a non-empty **Y column** (worktree vs index) or `??` untracked path in `git status --porcelain -uall` fails, while an **X-column staged entry is the normal state of index scope and never fails**. **A directional mismatch ⇒ FAIL (exit 1); usage / environment errors (including a failing `git status`) ⇒ exit 2**. An optional external JSON (`GATES_CHANGESET_JSON`) that disagrees with the derived values is a usage error (exit 2) and **must never pass silently**. |
| G6 | **Ledger metadata consistency** | The ledger metadata of changed lines must be self-consistent: status tense (G6-1), cost clause (G6-2), run-id format (G6-3), expiring literal (G6-4), section-reference resolution (G6-5), status-word closed set (G6-6), ID reconciliation (G6-7), DR-number uniqueness and membership (G6-8). Scope = changed lines (the added lines of `git diff -U0`); **historical rows are not retroactive** (same as G1-a / G3 / G4). |
| G7 | **Review-bundle completeness** | A changed `docs/validation/*.md` must carry a fenced block (info string `artifacts`) of `<repo-relative path><TAB><disposition>` rows, the dispositions being `repo` (may be omitted), `local-only` and `missing-historical`; every finding row needs a disposition cell and a stopped state needs `已停止——未完成`. **Structure only**: artifact content, run reachability and number truth are NOT judged. **Verdict line (G7-e, added v1.33, landed by OB-42)**: a changed line's verdict line must be in the §4.1 closed-set enum - under a closed-set prefix the value must belong to that prefix's enumerated set; a **bare token** (no prefix) and a **table-external prefix** (an uppercase prefix carrying exactly one closed-set value) are format deviations, as is a residual `RE_` placeholder. **Self-reference guard**: a line carrying any of `不得残留` / `示例` / `引文` / `冲突` / `矛盾` is skipped. **Stated boundary**: a prefix whose value is unrecognisable (a gate reading such as `SELFTEST: PASS 362/362`) is **not judged**; the **existence and position** of the verdict line (4.1 requires "the last line") are **not mechanically covered** (except inside a `sixstage` fenced block, see G7-f) and are carried by ⑤⑥⑦. **⑥ output contract (G7-f, added v1.46)**: see the G7-f sub-rule in this section. |
| G8 | **Frozen evidence-pack self-consistency** | when the change set touches `docs/validation/evidence/**`: (i) shape guard - every changed path inside that tree must be a frozen-pack member (its nearest ancestor directory contains both `MANIFEST.md` and `SHA256SUMS.txt`) OR an evidence-root-level `*-freedom-list.md` OR an exact registration in `tools/gates/evidence-forms.txt`, otherwise FAIL; (ii) pack self-consistency - the `MANIFEST.md` archived-entry set ≡ the `SHA256SUMS.txt` entry set ≡ the set of payload files inside the pack (excluding the two metadata files), with each sha256 recomputed and matching its record (a missing list / a payload not listed / a hash mismatch / an uncommitted list conflicting with the in-pack files are each red; path separators are normalized to POSIX); (iii) encoding-normalization record - a `MANIFEST` carrying a "suffix and encoding mapping" section must hold both the original-byte sha256 and the archived-byte sha256 as two 64-bit hexadecimal values, and the archived value must match the `SHA256SUMS` record for that path (**a legacy pack without that section is exempt**); (iv) Rebuilt-archive annotation - a changed file that declares "rebuilt archive" in the **header annotation zone** must carry the rebuild basis (source path + basis + original-byte sha256 when available), and a missing basis is red. **Changed-set only** (scope = packs **touched** by the changed set; full in-pack self-consistency is intrinsic to this criterion and is not retroactive to untouched historical change sets); `MANIFEST` / `SHA256SUMS` parse failure, pack enumeration failure and a crippled whitelist are all FAIL or exit 2, and it **never passes silently**. |
| G9 | **Temp artifacts and `tmp/` references** | A "temp artifact committed into the repository" in the change set fails (static shape): (1) the basename matches `.log / .tmp / .bak / .out` (outside `tmp/`); (2) the path starts with `tmp/` (**case-insensitive**) and is not `tmp/.gitkeep` (`tmp/` is gitignored, so a `git add -f` is the only way such a path reaches the change set; **force-added paths under OTHER gitignored roots are not covered by G9**). Exclusions: the evidence tree `docs/validation/evidence/**` (OB-79, owned by G8), deleted paths, and entries registered in `tools/gates/g9-whitelist.txt`; an empty change set reports INFO. **G9 does not cover the temporal shape** - `$env:TEMP`, locations outside the repository, PowerShell pipelines, and shell variables leave zero information in the CI input; G9 guards the same domain's **other shape** - a temp artifact committed into the repository; the temporal shape is registered as OB-99. **Relation to §5.1**: G9 is the mechanised criterion for the **static shape** of the §5.1 temp-artifact definition; the location closed set `tmp/<sliceID>/` and the out-of-repository prohibition still come from that definition. **Boundary 2**: a location violation that is not committed (the repository root / any location in the repository that is not `tmp/<sliceID>/`) **is not within G9's coverage** - G9 judges only the "committed into the repository" shape; an uncommitted location violation is borne by the `??` entries of ⑧c's `git status --porcelain` (see OB-100). **G9-e third predicate (added v1.40, landing OB-77, soft scan)**: every `tmp/<path>` token on an **added line** of the change set (at least one path segment; a bare `tmp/` is not judged) must resolve - `tree` / `index` = present on disk plus staged, `range` / `ci` = present at the scope endpoint; **an unresolvable reference reports INFO only and never fails** (one INFO per token plus one summary INFO, verdict unchanged). Exclusion-marker lines are skipped; `docs/validation/evidence/**` is excluded. **Clearing the historical dangling references belongs to the periodic full-ledger sweep (recorded state)**; this criterion guards added lines only. |

**G3 sub-rule (a.2 six-shape positional parity, added 2026-10-16, landed by OB-73)** **(a.1) pairing rule (v1.34, landing OB-90)**: a record is produced **from EITHER side** - an `_EN.md` change is always judged under its CN baseline path (an untouched baseline scores `+0/-0` ⇒ FAIL instead of silence); a baseline `.md` change pairs only when an `_EN` mirror **exists** (at the scope endpoint / on disk / in the same change set), so **a single-language document produces no record**; and an unreadable side of a pair (deleted / never present) **no longer skips the pair** - the numstat symmetry is still judged, and an incomparable directive pair makes `G3(a.2)` fail closed. **Only** the `docs/DELIVERY_DIRECTIVE.md` / `_EN` pair is **required** to have whole-file shape equality - the **six shapes** = line count / heading line numbers (positionally equal) / bold-marker count / pipe count / blank-line count / **backtick count**; any inequality ⇒ G3 fails ⇒ stop the commit. Division of labour with (a.1): (a.1) governs "changed-line-count symmetry", this item governs "whole-file shape parity"; other bilingual pairs are **not** required to have whole-file equality (they are historically asymmetric, so the rule would be unsatisfiable - the same **self-referential defect** argument as G4b). **Recorded-state ruling (v1.40, satisfiability pre-gate)**: the six shapes are all **whole-file count / position comparisons**, **not per-line**; two candidate extension shapes were evaluated and **downgraded to recorded state** - (1) **the seventh shape (CJK inter-character spaces)**: the CN directive yields **16** hits, each judged an **intentional form** (set expressions / symbol chains / enumeration separators), so no equality criterion is set; (2) **the eighth shape (changed-line shape multiset)**: a full re-check of the 50 historical commits that touch the directive pair finds **13** of them (26%) with a CN≠EN added/deleted-line triplet multiset (e.g. the same table row carries extra backticks on the EN side, `0/3/6` vs `0/3/8`) ⇒ the criterion is unsatisfiable; **the earlier "4 sampled / 2 unsatisfiable" figure was an erroneous estimate and is void** (OB-104). **Either item may be revived only after its satisfiability is solved**; until then, cross-line compensating drift and both shapes are carried by manual positional review and ⑦. **Origin**: OB-73 measured a backtick-level CN / `_EN` drift (recomputed on the `git show HEAD` baseline: the same 7 places, delta +2 ⇒ a **pre-existing drift, not introduced by this slice**); this slice performs **shape alignment only** (add / remove backticks, no content rewrite) and registers each place in the validation report.

**Criterion-scope exclusion (2026-10-05, OB-79 landing)**: the **target file set** of G1-a / G2 / G4a / G4b / G6 excludes `docs/validation/evidence/**` (**isomorphic** to G7's existing exclusion and **single-sourced** in the implementation layer); changed files inside that tree are **carried by G8**. **Reason**: the evidence tree holds **frozen historical artifacts** whose content cannot satisfy criteria written for "a new document" (G1-a table concatenation, G2 placeholders, G4a BOM+LF, G4b anomalous characters and G6 ledger metadata all assume "newly written by this slice"); **first instance** = the slice `DIRECTIVE-TMP-LIFECYCLE` produced **8 red criteria** after archiving historical `.md` files, **all inside that tree**, with **0 failures** outside it (see OB-79 in §12.4). **Criterion scope and post-freeze appends (OB-41, v1.33)**: a criterion's "changed-line set" is always **the diff of the current operation**; when an append is made to an **already-frozen version** (freeze ruling / CI result write-back) the scope **degenerates** to that append by definition and is **not comparable** with the freeze-period reading ⇒ the report must state the scope, the **freeze reading file must stay untouched** and the new reading is stored separately (measured on `v1.12`: 1097 references → 14).

**Known boundaries of G8 (stated truthfully)**: **(i) G8 is a shape-level guard** - it only checks "the shape of paths inside the evidence tree and the pack's internal self-consistency", and does not judge content semantics; **(ii) "live code masquerading as a frozen pack" is not detected by G8** - a frozen pack legitimately carries script-type artifacts (precedent: the `*.ps1` files under `docs/validation/evidence/b2-2026-09-17/machine-ops/`), and that residual risk is carried by the ⑤⑥⑦ review chain, so it must not be claimed that G8 covers it. **(iii) the encoding form of payloads is not verified by G8** - G4a already excludes that tree, and G8-c only checks the "suffix and encoding mapping" line **recorded** in the `MANIFEST`; for a pack **without that section** (a legacy pack), the encoding form of its payload has **no criterion coverage**, and that residual **strength gap** is carried by the ⑤⑥⑦ review chain.

**Sub-rule G1-b (version metadata consistency, added 2026-09-21)**: **the version number declared in the file header must equal the version number on the latest line of the §0.3 change log**, and the header date must match the latest line's date. Mechanical criterion: extract `版本：vX.Y` from the file header, **extract `vX.Y` of the last row only from within the §0.3 table range** (**must not scan the full text** — other tables (e.g. the v3.1 comparison table in appendix C.1) also contain the `| vN.N |` form, and a full-text scan yields 5 false hits, measured), **the two must be equal** (not equal ⇒ G1 fails ⇒ stop the commit).
> **Origin**: an instance of "header v1.3 out of sync with log v1.4" appeared in this slice (⑥ round 2); ⑥'s Section D explicitly pointed out that "version metadata consistency" was previously **not within the mechanical coverage of G1–G5** ⇒ this sub-rule is exactly that missing coverage.

**Hard rule R6.2**: if any of G1–G9 fails ⇒ **stop the commit**; after fixing, re-run all gates.

**Scripting status (stated truthfully, updated 2026-10-19, v1.40)**: `tools/gates/gate.js` is the single entry point (`--all` (optionally `--skip-base` to run only the criteria; that switch combines **only** with `--all`, and every other combination including `--help` exits 2) / `--check=<ID>` / `--list` / `--selftest`, `--scope=tree|index|range:<A>..<B>|ci`; exit code 0 = every executed criterion PASS, 1 = at least one FAIL, 2 = usage / environment error). **Scripted**: G1-a, G1-b, **G1 (since v1.36)**, G2, G3(a.1) / G3(a.2) / G3(b) (the sub-checks are individually selectable), G4a, G4b, G5-a(1)(2), G5-b, **G5 (since v1.36)**, G5-c, G5-d (since v1.40), R2.5, G6-1 to G6-8, G7-a to G7-f, G8-a to G8-d, **G9 (since v1.38; G9-e since v1.40)**, plus the four base gates of §6.1 (gofmt / build / vet / test). **No main rule prints `SKIP` any more**; the **recorded-as-unmechanised** boundaries of G1 / G5 (which sub-items remain human judgements) are written into the corresponding §6.3 rows, and `--check=G1` / `--check=G5` select them individually. CI appends a step at the end of the `test` job that runs this script (first `--selftest`, then `--all --scope=ci`); that step **must not** carry `continue-on-error`, and a first-run red must be fixed to green. Concurrent scheduling (v1.44, OB-113 sub-items 1 and 2): under `--all` the criteria start concurrently and their records are written back in `RUN_ORDER`, so the output order never changes; every criterion is still synchronous today, so this only paves the way - the real speed-up waits for a later slice to make the slow criteria async; the criterion output of `--all --skip-base` equals the criterion part of `--all` byte for byte (only the four base records are omitted); it is **not** byte-for-byte equivalent to the per-criterion `--check` runs, because `--check` prefix-matches (for instance `--check=G5-d` also emits the aggregate `G5` record), so no equivalence is claimed. This line must not be read as "fully automatic".

**G5 sub-rule G5-a (role-file frontmatter hard gate, added 2026-09-21; hardened the same day per ⑦ round 3)**: the frontmatter of `prfrail-*.agent.md` **must not contain the `model` key** (writing an empty value makes the carrier **fail to load entirely**; see §2.6 / §9.3). Mechanical criteria (**both must be satisfied**): ① `grep -l "^model:" .github/agents/prfrail-*.agent.md` **must produce no output**; ② the frontmatter of **all** `.agent.md` files **must not contain an empty `model`** — the three forms `^\s*model\s*:\s*$`, `model: ""`, `model: ''` all count as empty, and all must have **0 hits** (coverage includes non-`prfrail-*` files and whitespace variants). Violation ⇒ G5 fails ⇒ **stop the commit**. It is a mandatory check item once scripted. **Exit-code routing (OB-20, v1.33)**: for ①, `git grep` **exit 1 (no match) means satisfied**, while **any other non-zero exit (including 127 with git absent and 128 with a non-repository) is a usage / environment error ⇒ exit code 2**, and **must not be conflated with "zero hits"**.

> **Origin**: ⑦ round 2 pointed out that "the F3 defense rests only on manual pre-check" ⇒ G5-a was added; ⑦ round 3 further pointed out that the original criteria **matched only a fixed form and covered only `prfrail-*`** (whitespace variants and other role files were missed) ⇒ this clause is hardened accordingly.

**G5 sub-rule G5-b (role-file `model` value gate, added 2026-09-21, changed to a whitelist closed set on 2026-10-03)**: for **newly added or modified** `.github/agents/*.agent.md`, the value of the `model` field (**if present**) **must belong to the whitelist closed set** — the closed set = `deepseek-v4-pro`, `deepseek-flash`, `mai-code-1.1-flash`, `gpt-5.3-codex`, `gpt-6.1-sol`, `gpt-6-luna`, `glm-5.3`, `glm-5.3-flash` (matching the §2.2 `modelId` (internal identifier) column; **case-sensitive, verbatim equality**, both ends trimmed before comparison, and a quoted value is not verbatim equal). Mechanical criterion: parse the frontmatter block only and extract the values of **all** `model` keys in it; **any value outside the closed set ⇒ FAIL**; a missing key ⇒ PASS (§2.6); an empty value ⇒ judged by G5-a ②. Violation ⇒ G5 fails ⇒ **stop the commit**.
> **Origin (root fix for OB-8)**: measurement on 2026-09-21 found that the default `model` of 8 existing role files belonged to the blacklist (when `model` is not passed explicitly, the black is reachable directly via `agentName`). The user's ruling adopted the combination of "**delete the default value + hard gate**" (**do not delete the files**): first perform a **field-deletion probe** on one inactive file (delete the `model` line → invoke that carrier: **it loads and works normally**); after the probe passes, delete the `model` field from all 8 blacklist files; this G5-b clause then prevents its re-introduction.

**G5 sub-rule G5-c (appendix-B call strings in the 2.2 qualified-name set, added v1.33, landed by OB-29)**: for a `model='<qualified name>'` call string on a **changed line** inside appendix B, the value (**after stripping whitespace and backticks**) must be a member of the **qualified-name column of section 2.2 in the same file** (that column is the authority and the criterion reads it from the document itself, so editing 2.2 moves the judged set with it and the two cannot drift); a value outside the set ⇒ G5 fails ⇒ stop the commit. **Scope**: changed lines inside the appendix B region only (the same discipline as G6 / G7-c / G7-d); historical lines are not re-judged. **Fail-closed**: an appendix B region whose 2.2 qualified-name column cannot be read (missing heading or header, or an empty column) ⇒ throw `UsageError` (exit 2) - never a silent pass. **Origin**: OB-29 (appendix B's `model=` value set previously had no mechanical criterion; the wording was synced from the `modelId` column to the qualified-name column when R2.2 landed).

**G5 sub-rule G5-d (role-file frontmatter five keys, added v1.40, landing OB-30)**: an **added or modified** `.github/agents/*.agent.md` in the change set must carry **exactly once** each of the five §2.6 keys - `name` / `description` / `tools` / `user-invocable` / `target`; a missing key, a duplicate, an empty value (`key:` / `key: ""` / `key: ''`) or absent frontmatter (not opening with `---` or unterminated) ⇒ FAIL. **Extra keys are not judged** - the shape and value of `model` are owned by G5-a / G5-b. Scope = added / modified paths of the change set (deleted paths are not judged); an unreadable target file ⇒ `UsageError` (exit code 2), never a silent pass. **Existing-deviation note**: the missing `user-invocable` in `sol-orchestrator.agent.md` is a **historical deviation**, **not judged** while outside the change set, and must be completed when that file is modified. **The Appendix D role-file template does not land in this slice** - the criterion comes first, and the template is left to a later role-file governance slice.

**Sub-rule G1-a (table structural integrity, added 2026-09-21)**: **any line starting with `|` must occupy its own line**; two table records must not be concatenated into one line (affects: change log, status table, observation item ledger, and the tables in appendix C). Mechanical criteria (two independent commands):

1. `grep -n "^|.*||" <file>` against **the lines added/modified in this change set** **must produce no output** (**scope = changed lines, not the full text**, same as G3/G4; full-text comparison would report historical legacy defects as this slice's defects);
2. compare **column counts** table block by table block (before counting, **first strip escaped `\|`** — otherwise a `\|` inside a code span causes false positives (measured 2026-09-21: 4 false positives without stripping, 0 after stripping).

> **Origin**: the same "table-row concatenation" defect **appeared twice** within this slice (§0.3 change log, §12.4 observation item ledger), and both were **found independently** by ⑥ and ⑦ ⇒ the defect **cannot be reliably caught by human eyeballing** and must be mechanized (⑦ round 2 R3 and ⑥'s two rounds of conclusions).
>
> **Known pre-existing exception (not fixed in this slice)**: the M6/M7 rows at `docs/t027/A6_PILOT_LAUNCH.md:269` have the `||` concatenation **already in HEAD** (verified with `git show HEAD:...` as **not introduced by this slice**); because the SW-1 boundary requires historical drafts to **only have a header pointer added, with the body unchanged**, it is **not fixed**, merely **registered as a pre-existing defect**, and does not block this slice's commit.

**G6 sub-rule (ledger metadata consistency, added 2026-09-22)**: the four G6 sub-checks judge **changed lines only** - the **added lines** of `git diff -U0` (in tree scope an untracked file counts in full, a staged-only file is taken with `--cached`), **the same scope as G1-a / G3 / G4**; **historical rows are never retroactive** (a committed old line never condemns this slice). Target files: every `.md` in the change set.

**Self-reference guard**: lines carrying any exclusion marker (`不得残留` / `示例` / `引文` / `冲突` / `矛盾`) are skipped by every G6 sub-check - isomorphic to G2's own guard. ⇒ A sentence in this rule that quotes a forbidden shape or historical text must carry one of those markers, otherwise the gate judges the directive itself. **Bilingual obligation (OB-18, v1.33)**: the marker set **stays the five CN words** and **is not extended to English synonyms** (`example` / `quote` / `conflict` are ordinary words in English prose, and extending the set would exempt a large surface by accident); therefore, when the `_EN` mirror quotes a forbidden form verbatim, the line **must itself carry at least one of those CN markers** (keeping `示例` in the English line is the existing convention; an EN line without a marker was measured to turn G6-3 red).

**G6-1 (status tense, catches the OB-11 class)**: a changed line carries a pending marker (`待` plus one of ①–⑩ / 终审 / 独立扫描 / 复审 / 预审 / 盲审 / 提交授权), and **the same block** (between two adjacent `^#{2,4} ` headings) contains a **same-label** completion marker (the 实得 parenthesised form / bold 实得 / 已闭合 / ✅ 已完成) ⇒ FAIL. The completion marker must share the label and fall inside its 40-character window, otherwise a block-level "✅ 已完成" would kill any pending line. **示例行 (example line)**: 待 ⑥ 独立扫描 coexisting with ⑥ 独立扫描（实得）PASS in one block ⇒ FAIL.
> **Origin**: OB-11's C.0e status-line tense conflict (待 ⑥ 独立扫描 vs ⑥ 独立扫描实得 PASS below); SW-5 missed 4 more of the same class before closing ⇒ human eyeballing is unreliable. **Measured evidence (引文 / quotation)**: `fb12ff9` reads 待 ⑦ 终审 plus ⑥ 实得 below ⇒ **no false positive** (no same-label completion marker).

**G6-2 (cost clause, catches the A1 class)**: the **realized clause** of a changed line (`⑥×n` / `⑦×n` tokens, with `×` / `x` / `X` all accepted as the multiplication sign, or a `= n 次` total folded in only when the line already carries a qualifying ⑥ / ⑦ token - otherwise the line is not compared) and the **same-key** realized clause found **anywhere in the file** must have identical token vectors; otherwise ⇒ FAIL (both positions and both vectors are printed). Clause qualification uses the **nearest qualifier**: the **closest** `预算` within the 60 characters before the token ⇒ budget clause (never compared); the nearest `实得` or **bare `成本`** ⇒ realized clause. ⇒ Do not degrade to a "预算 appeared somewhere in the window" containment test (that lets a preceding budget keyword shadow a following realized clause and makes the comparison vacuous). Slice keys: `SW-n` / `DR-n` / `OB-n` / `GATES-EXT` / uppercase-letter-plus-digits on the line, otherwise the same-shaped id in the enclosing block heading.
> **Basis (A1)**: the DR-FIX §0.3 v1.9 row and the appendix C.0e cost row conflict on the **same key** (`SW-5`) - 引文 (quotation): 成本 ⑥×1 + ⑦×1 versus 实得 ⑥×1 + ⑦×2 = 3 次. **Measured evidence**: `--scope=range:b738e6b^..b738e6b` must be red; `--scope=range:9f68a52^..9f68a52` (after the fix) must be green, matching the fix DR-FIX actually applied.

**G6-3 (run-id format, format only)**: the legal shape is `run` plus a space plus **9–13 digits** (this repository's run ids are 11 digits; the constant can be adjusted by a later slice and verified by the T-series tests). No reachability, no network (user ruling). **A digit run is judged only when it is NOT immediately followed by a word** - an ASCII letter or a CJK ideograph; a run followed by a word is a prose count (such as `run 1234 times` / `连续 run 12 次`), not a run id. A digit run that is **backticked, or at end of line / before punctuation** (`run 111`, `run 1234。`, `` `run 1234` ``) is still judged **only when it is also NOT followed by a word**; when it IS followed by a word it is not judged (backticks alone do not judge it: `` `run 1234 times` ``). **Every G6-3 rule (the placeholder rules included) additionally carries a leading boundary**: `run` immediately preceded by a word character is not judged (`rerun 1234`, `prerun 12`, `在run 1234`). The placeholder forms (angle brackets / word list / CJK wording) carry no following-word restriction and are checked in **ANY** context (the leading boundary still applies). **Angle-bracket narrowing (OB-17, v1.33)**: the angle-bracket rule fires when the bracketed token **starts with an uppercase letter** (e.g. `<RUN_ID>`); **CLI usage examples** (e.g. `run <chain-file>`, whose token starts lowercase) **are not judged**. **示例行 (example line; any of these shapes ⇒ FAIL - an all-zero run id fails at any length)**: `run 0` / `run 000000` / `run <RUN_ID>` / `run TBD` / `run N/A` / `run TODO` / `run 待定` / `run 占位…` / `run 7` / `run 12345678` / `run 123456789012345`.
> **Known residual false positive (registered, not fixed - G1-a precedent)**: the angle-bracket placeholder rule also catches CLI usage examples - two instances exist: `prfrail run <chain-file>` at `docs/RFC-proofrail-unattended-ai-engineering-product.md:737` and `:1085`; because only changed lines are judged this **does not affect the gate today**, but either line would produce a spurious red as soon as it is touched. **No whitelist entry was added** (a whitelist entry would mask this shape); registered as **OB-17**. This line carries the 示例 marker so that the quoted shape does not trip the rule (see OB-18).

**G6-4 (expiring literal)**: a changed line carrying a line-count / file-count / aggregate-count literal (such as `NN/NN 行`, `NN 行`, `NN 个文件`, `共 NN`) ⇒ FAIL; the whitelist may exempt them one by one (reason and context excerpt mandatory). **示例行 (example line)**: 995/995 行、共 128 个文件。
> **Origin (引文 / quotation)**: the OB-11 lesson - a ledger must not carry expiring literals (line counts / file counts), only criteria such as "verified consistent by G3/G4" (v1.9 removed 995/995 accordingly).

**G6-5 (section-reference resolution, added 2026-09-23, landing OB-32)**: on the added lines of the change set that sit inside the §0.3 change-log table interval or the appendix C ledger interval (from C.0 up to, excluding, `### C.1`), every §X.Y reference must **exist exactly** in the heading set of **this file and its `_EN` mirror** (`^#{2,4} ` headings + leading bold labels such as §1.7.1 / §3.4.1), or resolve via a **parent-section prefix** (§7.2 resolves through §7.2.1). **Approximate-criterion statement**: this sub-check covers only the "dangling reference" subclass and **does not cover** C1's full class of "the log claims a change while the body has no such content" (content-level reconciliation stays with ⑤ ⑥ ⑦ and G6-1 / G6-2, see OB-32 in §12.4); the v3.1 comparison table in Appendix C.1 and C.2 / C.3 are **outside the target region**; an external document's section number is exempted by an exclusion-marked line or by `g6-whitelist.txt`. **Endpoint self-check (added v1.33, landed by OB-46)**: a region boundary is a **premise** of judging and must not degrade silently - (1) `### C.0` present with no `### C.1` delimiter (an unterminated region) ⇒ throw `UsageError` (exit 2), refusing to judge an unbounded region and never re-admitting the C.1 comparison table or C.2 / C.3; (2) the directive pair must yield both the §0.3 region and the appendix C region, and a missing one ⇒ `UsageError` (a renamed heading must not yield a PASS). **示例行 (example line, any of these shapes ⇒ FAIL)**: `| v1.13 | … | 新增 §7.9 评审收敛判据 | … |`.

**G6-6 (ledger status closed set, added 2026-09-23, landing OB-38)**: the disposition cell's **first status word** of a changed row inside the §12.4 table interval (a `| OB-N |` line) must be ∈ the closed set `观察中 / 待办 / 待议 / 已处置 / 已裁决 / 已登记` (the `_EN` mirror uses `Observing / To do / To be discussed / Resolved / Ruling / Registered`; **word** may be followed by a parenthetical note). **示例行 (example line, any of these shapes ⇒ FAIL)**: `| OB-32 | … | **下一片必做**（…）|`. **由来 (引文 / quotation)**: P5 measured that §12.4's status vocabulary and G6-1's marker vocabulary barely intersect ⇒ ledger status conflicts previously had no mechanical coverage; this sub-check is independent of G6-1 (block-level semantics are incompatible, **no widening** of G6-1, argued in §7.8 and this subsection). **Declared language (added v1.33, landed by OB-47)**: the word table is not picked by the file-name suffix but by the **language identifier declared in the document header** (`lang:zh` selects the CN table, `lang:en` the EN table, within the header window, implementation constant `LANG_HEADER_WINDOW`); a missing declaration, an identifier outside the closed set, or a declaration that disagrees with the file-name suffix (any rename triggers it) all throw `UsageError` (exit 2) - never a silent table switch, never silent degradation. (Scope: the declaration is required only when the file's §12.4 interval actually carries ledger rows to judge; a same-shaped §12.4 heading in another document is skipped rather than forced. G6-5 carries no word table; its mirror relation is a structural clause and is out of this item's scope.) **Whole-token strict matching (v1.34, landing OB-91; supersedes the v1.33 exact-value sentence)**: a tag starts at every `lang:` occurrence and **consumes the maximal run of continuation characters** (letters / digits / `_` / `.` / `-` / `:`, a closed enumeration; **the terminator set is its complement**, so a space / tab / comma / backtick / CJK punctuation / end of line all end the token), and that token is then compared **whole** against the closed set - only `lang:zh` and `lang:en` are legal; any expression that is not one of those literals (`lang:zh-cn` / `lang:en-us` / `lang:zh_cn` / `lang:en_us` / a bare `lang:` / `lang:ZH` / `lang:zh-` / `lang:zh.` / `lang:zh2`) always takes the `unknown language tag` `UsageError` path, **including when a legal tag is present as well** (fail-closed coexistence), and is **never silently accepted** as zh / en. **Second-colon forms (v1.40, landing OB-97)**: `:` belongs to the continuation set ⇒ `lang:zh:en` / `lang:zh:` are consumed whole as a **single token**, are not equal to any closed-set literal ⇒ they always take the `unknown language tag` `UsageError` path and are never judged as zh / en by their prefix. **Coverage invariant**: every `lang:` occurrence inside the header window is consumed by exactly one judgement (an occurrence preceded by a word character, such as `xlang:zh`, **is not an occurrence**) ⇒ no `lang:`-prefixed form can escape judgement (the v1.33 charset patch could always be outgrown by the next delimiter; this is the structural fix).

**G6-7 (declaration-line ↔ ledger ID reconciliation, added v1.40, landing OB-32)**: scope = the added lines of the **§0.3 change-log region only**; the judged object = an ID key on the line (`OB-\d+` / `SW-\d+` / `DR-\d+` / `GATES-[A-Z]+`) lying inside the **±40-character window** of a declaration verb; the declaration-verb closed set = CN `新增 / 登记 / 补登 / 收口 / 翻 / 处置 / 更正 / 闭合 / 落地`, EN `added / registered / closed / resolved / corrected / landed / flipped`; evidence = the **whole §12.4 ledger region or appendix C ledger region** (the judging line never serves as its own evidence); an ID absent from every ledger region ⇒ FAIL. **Negated-verb guard**: a verb immediately preceded by `不` / `未` / `无` (or `not` / `no`) is not a declaration (e.g. 「本片不新增」); **exclusion-marker lines are skipped** (the five CN markers, same self-reference guard as G6); `g6-whitelist.txt` exempts one finding per registration. **No-ledger premise**: a document with neither a §12.4 region nor an appendix C region has nothing to reconcile against and is **not judged**. **Region endpoint self-check inherited from G6-5** (the two OB-46 assertions): `### C.0` present with no `### C.1` delimiter ⇒ `UsageError`; the **directive pair** must yield both the §0.3 region and the appendix C region, and a missing one ⇒ `UsageError`. **Approximate-criterion statement**: this sub-check mechanises only the subclass "an ID-bearing claim ↔ a ledger row"; **wording-level claims without an ID are still carried by ⑤⑥⑦ and G6-5 and must not be read as full-class C1 mechanisation**.

**G6-8 (DR-number uniqueness and membership, added v1.46, the DR-ledger primary-key guard)**: scope = **four structured number-declaration positions** - A: the id column of the defect table in `docs/t027/REMAINING_SLICES{,_EN}.md`, shaped `| **DR-n** |`; B: the cumulative table of the same two files, `| DR-n …` rows; C: `| DR-n …` table rows in `docs/validation/*.md`; D: entry-line prefixes in `docs/validation/*.md`, `- **DR-n（…）**` (anchored on the opening parenthesis; whitespace between the number and the bracket is **allowed**, so `- **DR-n (…)` and `- **DR-n（…` are judged the same way). The number token carries left / right boundaries (an `ADR`-prefixed number is not truncated to `DR`); numbers of three digits or more are **asymmetric**: at a reference position they produce no number token (silently not judged), whereas at a minting position (an id cell shaped as three digits) they **explicitly FAIL** and are annotated as out of bounds for the ADR domain. **Primary-key set** = all rows of both mirrored defect tables ∪ the seeds `{1, 8, 10}`; **judgement**: a duplicate number inside the primary-key set, or a number at a declaration position that is not in it ⇒ **FAIL**; changed lines only, historical lines never re-judged. **Uniqueness scope**: the same number minted twice **inside one file** ⇒ FAIL; **dual mint (v1.46 M2)**: a changed id-column row must also appear as an id-column row **in the other mirror** - a missing side ⇒ FAIL with the detail naming which side is missing; when the OTHER mirror is **unreadable** the face is **skipped** (the verdict is unchanged) but the detail must carry `dual-mint: other mirror unreadable, face skipped` (a stated, never silent, fail-open); `G3(a)` judges only numstat line-count symmetry for `docs/t027/**` and never id agreement, so mirror **id agreement** is carried by this check (measured: the two defect tables carry the same id set `{2,3,4,5,6,7,9,11}`, so the rule is satisfiable by the corpus). **Seed provenance**: the seeds `{1, 8, 10}` stand for "the known defect where a historical slice name reused a number", "the `DR-8` token on the entry line `docs/validation/directive-gates-debt-sweep.md:78` (that line matches FACE D ⇒ once it changes, every token on it needs membership; the evidence tree is excluded by OB-79 and is never judged, so 'the flake candidate inside the evidence tree' is NOT its provenance)" and "the `DR-10` entry-row registration in that same file"; adding or removing a seed must be a **visible diff**. **Capability boundary (stated truthfully)**: this check catches **colliding numbers, out-of-range numbers and one-sided mints at declaration positions**, and **does not catch** semantic mismatches at reference positions (the same number pointing at different defects in different documents) or collisions in free text; **a parenthetical or prose passage is not a declaration position** (once a structured row is recognised, every number token on that row is judged). **Grading**: FAIL only, no advisory.

**G7 sub-rule (review-bundle completeness, added 2026-09-22)**: target files = `docs/validation/*.md` in the change set (**excluding** `docs/validation/evidence/**`); the rule text lives inside the directive, outside the scan domain ⇒ the self-reference trap is **structurally immune**. **Structure only**: artifact content, run reachability, number truth and report-conclusion truth are NOT judged.
**Machine contract**: a changed validation report must carry a fenced block (triple-backtick fence, info string `artifacts`) of `<repo-relative path><TAB><disposition>` rows; the dispositions are `repo` (may be omitted, the default), `local-only` and `missing-historical`. **Self-compliance**: this slice's own validation report (`docs/validation/gates-ext{,_EN}.md`) must carry that block.
**Sub-checks**: G7-a a missing fence ⇒ FAIL; G7-b an unresolvable disposition / path (`repo` or omitted, the same meaning in every scope: `git cat-file -e <endpoint>:<path>` for range / ci; for tree / index the path must be staged in the index (`git ls-files --error-unmatch`) AND present on disk ⇒ a tree-scope run is a faithful pre-flight only after `git add` of the declared artifact) or an unknown disposition token ⇒ FAIL, while `local-only` and `missing-historical` are accepted as declared; G7-c a changed finding row (line starting with `|` plus a bold Critical/High/Medium/Low) whose **last cell** (the disposition cell) is empty or carries no disposition token ⇒ FAIL (structural only: the token must sit in the row's **last cell**; a disposition word inside the description cell does not count - `| **High** | ledger says “Fixed” but the disposition cell is empty | |` FAILs; the accepted token set is the CN / EN union - `已修` / `已整改` / `已闭合` / `已处置` / `接受` / `拒收` / `记录在案` / `待办` / `观察中` / `待议` / `已停止`, plus `Fixed` / `Resolved` / `Closed` / `Accepted` / `Rejected` / `Deferred` / `Recorded` / `To do` / `Observed` / `To be discussed` / `Not a problem` / `Documented`); G7-d a changed status line on an added line (`状态` or `Status` followed by a separator) declaring a stopped state (the token set is the CN / EN union, with ASCII tokens matched case-insensitively: `已停止` / `停止态` / `Stopped` / `Halted`) without an incomplete mark (`已停止——未完成` / `Stopped — incomplete`) on the same line or in the same block ⇒ FAIL (added lines only, never the whole report; a prose line that merely names the rule is not a status line, avoiding a self-reference false positive).

**G7-f (⑥ output contract, added v1.46, the mechanical check of ⑧a's transcription)**: target files = `docs/validation/*.md` in the change set (**excluding** `docs/validation/evidence/**`); it inspects triple-backtick fenced blocks whose info string is `sixstage`. **Judgement**: the block contains the five sections `Section A`–`Section E`, complete and in order, and all five appear as level 2–4 headings (`##`–`####`) whose text **starts with `Section X`** (descriptive text may follow, e.g. `## Section A Scope and method`; `Section AA` / `Subsection A` are not recognised); **multiple** conclusion lines are allowed but all values must be **equal**; exactly one must itself be the block's **last non-blank line**, whose `INDEPENDENT SCAN` prefix value ∈ the §4.1 closed set; exactly two must sit one at the **first non-blank line** after the `## Section A` heading (the §7.2.2-A overall verdict line) and one at the block's last non-blank line (each of the two values must also be in the §4.1 closed set); three or more ⇒ FAIL (the detail lists every line), unequal values ⇒ FAIL (the detail lists each line and its value), and a position outside the pair ⇒ FAIL - locating only the first one lets `conclusion → note → conclusion` escape, and the position constraint is exactly what closes that hole (**only when the block's last non-blank line is a conclusion line**, a position violation and an unequal-value violation coexist with the **unequal value** winning and the position violation producing no extra finding; when the second conclusion line is not the block-final line the block FAILs as "content after the block-final conclusion line"; the number of findings is not a judgement caliber - only whether any FAIL exists); the `SEVERITY` line of Section B agrees with the conclusion line (a SEVERITY line ⇔ a `FINDINGS` conclusion line) (the **BLOCK-FINAL** conclusion value is used when the two values differ); **how a SEVERITY line is recognised (v1.46)**: a table row (leading `|`) is a SEVERITY row when **any cell** equals `Critical`/`High`/`Medium`/`Low` after stripping surrounding blanks and `*` (this covers both the `| High | … |` first-column shape and the `| # | SEVERITY | … |` shape the ⑥ layer actually writes); a legacy line that does not start with `|` falls back to **first-word** equality after stripping the leading `|` and `*`, with a table pipe `|` required, and the two rules are **unioned**; `UNCOVERED` is not a severity token; `UNCOVERED` is not a severity token; **exclusion-marker reuse (v1.46)**: the marker scan of ① and the SEVERITY scan of Section B **both** skip lines carrying any of the five `G7-e` exclusion markers (`不得残留` / `示例` / `引文` / `冲突` / `矛盾`) - one constant, never a copied list - so a quoted marker or a quoted severity row is never judged; nothing may follow the **block-final** conclusion line (notes always go outside the fence). **Four states when no fenced block is present (v1.46, the transition rule for historical reports)**: ① the report mentions `INDEPENDENT SCAN` with no waiver declaration at all ⇒ **FAIL** (self-contradictory: the scan was never transcribed); ② the fixed skip declaration (regex `^-\s*⑥\s*(?:跳过|skipped)\s*[：:]\s*\S`, i.e. `- ⑥ 跳过：<理由>`) ⇒ **PASS**; ③ the fixed historical-exemption declaration (regex `^-\s*⑥\s*(?:历史豁免|historical exemption)\s*[：:]\s*\S`, i.e. `- ⑥ 历史豁免：<理由>`) ⇒ **PASS**, with the detail naming the **historical-exemption channel**; ④ neither a marker nor any declaration ⇒ **INFO** (existing reports only; a newly added report with neither fence nor a valid skip declaration ⇒ **FAIL**, see (b)). **Waiver preconditions (v1.46)**: **(a) a NON-EMPTY reason** - both declaration regexes end in `[：:]\s*\S`, so the fixed form alone is not a declaration; a blank reason means the declaration **does not stand** and the line is treated as absent, falling through to the states above (the verdict may then be FAIL or INFO, never a pass produced by the blank line). **(b) the fence obligation of a newly added report (revised v1.46)** - when the report is **added by this change set**, it must carry the `sixstage` fence or a ⑥ skip declaration with a NON-EMPTY reason; with neither it **FAILs** (the detail states that obligation); `历史豁免` is invalid for a newly added report (a report born inside the change set has no history to exempt ⇒ **FAIL**); a marker still **FAILs** despite a skip declaration (see the order of judgement below); newness is read from the change-set record own git status (untracked or status letter `A` ⇒ added; `M` / `R` / `D` ⇒ not added; no record ⇒ treated as not added, so missing data never tightens the verdict). **Order of judgement (revised v1.46)**: the newly-added / existing split comes first - the new-report guard runs **before** the historical exemption and the skip declaration, and inside the newly-added branch the order matches the existing one (historical exemption ⇒ marker ⇒ skip ⇒ neither); for an existing report the historical exemption is tested **before** the marker rule - a historical report carries `INDEPENDENT SCAN` by definition, so letting the marker rule win would make the channel unreachable; the skip declaration keeps its shipped precedence (a marker still **FAILs**). **Applicability**: the historical exemption is limited to reports committed before v1.46 whose verbatim transcription cannot be recovered; using it requires that fixed form with a reason inside the report, and a new slice (including any report this change set ADDS) must never use it (see §11.3). **The fence must be closed (v1.46)**: an unclosed `sixstage` fence ⇒ **FAIL**, and the detail gives the opening line number (an unterminated fence swallows the rest of the document, so its body cannot be a transcript and the three faces are not judged for that block). **Grading**: FAIL and INFO only, **no advisory** - it may never be disposed of as a "suggestion". **Capability boundary (stated truthfully)**: this check covers only **three faces inside** the `sixstage` fenced block (the five sections and their order / the conclusion line and its value / SEVERITY-vs-conclusion agreement); **the obligation that the fenced block exists** and "whether ⑥ should be enabled at all" still rest with §7.2.1 and ⑤⑦ for an existing report; for a newly added report, the existence of "the fence or a non-empty-reason skip declaration" is judged mechanically by this check since v1.46 (see (b)). **Scope precision (v1.46; touch definition v1.46)**: the check re-examines only the `sixstage` fenced blocks **touched by this change**; "touched" spans the WHOLE fence interval - **the opening and the closing fence line included** (a change that rewrites nothing but the opening or the closing line still enters judgement); unchanged pre-existing blocks are not re-judged.
> **Basis**: C1 of DR-FIX (a report conclusion with no artifact path ⇒ broken evidence chain) is caught by G7-a / G7-b; OB-10 ① (the stopped-state report header) **evaluation: adopted** as G7-d; OB-10 ② (the pilot-metrics slot of the kickoff message) **not adopted** (it exists only in session text and cannot be checked mechanically ⇒ OB-10 stays observing).

> **The four reports that predate this contract are not retroactively failed**: `docs/validation/dr-fix-enforcement-proxy{,_EN}.md` and `docs/validation/sw5-work-tiers{,_EN}.md` were committed before this contract existed ⇒ the criterion only looks at the change set, so it is **not retroactive** and **does not backfill** (they are history, see §11.1); the fence block is required only when they are changed next.
**Fixture convention**: `tools/gates/testdata/fixtures/*.txt` are mapped to virtual `docs/**/*.md` paths by `selftest.js` ⇒ the criteria still select `.md` targets, yet the tool **never** trips on its own deliberate violations; fixtures are deliberately not stored with a `.md` suffix and an assertion in `selftest.js` keeps that convention.
**Two whitelists**: `tools/gates/g6-whitelist.txt` (three mandatory fields: match string / file / reason plus context excerpt; one entry exempts one finding, a crippled entry ⇒ exit code 2) and `tools/gates/cjk-newwords.txt` (used by G4b; its role is unchanged and every character is still registered one by one). **Loader-source rule (GATES-EXT F1)**: `tree` / `index` read the working tree; `range` / `ci` read the scope's endpoint revision (`ci` means HEAD) - both whitelists share one loader, and each criterion prints the source it used in its `detail`.

### 6.4 CI Observation

After pushing, GitHub Actions must be observed and **reported back as double green**. A failed first run requires: ① obtain the raw log of the failing step; ② `gh run rerun --failed` to re-run and collect evidence; ③ determine whether it is flaky; ④ **register it as a defect item** (DR-N) stating the run number, failing step, failing test, error message, and re-run result. It is **forbidden** to cover up a red first run with "the re-run went green".

---

## 7. Review system

### 7.1 Three-layer review

| Layer | Role | Positioning | Key requirement |
|---|---|---|---|
| ⑤ | Pre-reviewer (preferred) | whether the implementation deviates from the plan | covers the plan item by item + the three classes of counter-examples |
| ⑥ | Independent scan (MAI) | low-cost supplementary scan | **Does not reference anyone else's checklist**; Section A–E |
| ⑦ | Independent final review (per §5.2 tier table) | the sole final-review gate | **The first round attaches no ⑤/⑥ checklist**; four-item review |

> **Note (⑧c is not one of the three review layers)**: the ⑧c wrap-up cleanup is executed **by the master** and **does not belong to** the ⑤⑥⑦ review layers - it is a fixed stage of §5.1 (see §5.1 / §3.4), parallel to ⑨ native validation.

### 7.2 ⑥ (independent scan layer)

### 7.2.1 Trigger

**Trigger**:
- **Behavior-changing slice** (contract / state machine / crash path / process governance / ownership semantics changed) → **enabled**;
- **Skippable** only when the following three conditions are **simultaneously** met: ① the change **lands only in documentation and configuration-wording files** (`.md`/comments/wording; non-machine-structural files such as `.gitignore`, `.txt`, `.html` count as this class); ② it contains no semantic change to `.go`/`.json`/`.yml`/schema/fixtures/**script logic**; ③ it **does not touch the semantics of the state machine, gate criteria, evidence model, or role/authorization contracts**. **Skipping must be confirmed by ⑦ or the user** (the master must not unilaterally declare it "skippable"), and the reason must be recorded in the report.

**Reading for skip-face evidence sufficiency (v1.41, landing OB-23; a different matter from the three conditions for skipping ⑥ in item ③ of this section, and neither refers to the other)**: the single decision standard = **equivalence coverage** - the substitute evidence must equivalently cover the production semantics of the skipped face, and it **must be explicitly declared** (which face was skipped / what substitute evidence / which equivalence tier). Three equivalence tiers: **(i) mechanical-criterion substitution** (the skipped face is covered by a G1–G9 or §6.1 criterion) **is not exempt from argument** and must carry a structured statement: (a) **which aspects** of the substituted item the criterion covers; (b) **which aspects it misses** (claiming "no omission" requires a reason); ⑦ re-checks that statement and judges "the criterion is indeed equivalent", not "a criterion exists hence equivalent"; **(ii) review-layer substitution** (the face is independently carried by another review loop) ⇒ ⑤ must give the three-point equivalence argument (the skipped face / the substitute evidence / why it equivalently covers production semantics); **(iii) other substitution** (probes / focused tests / historical replay etc.) ⇒ equivalence is not presumed, **must** be labelled "non-equivalent substitute", and acceptance is ruled by ⑦ or the user. "Honest fail-closed" satisfies only the declaration duty and **does not constitute** a sufficiency argument. ⑦ has final discretion.
    - **Counter-example (measured 2026-09-21, must not be repeated)**: SW-1/SW-2 look like a "documentation slice" on the surface, but it **revised the §6.3 gate criteria and the evidence model** ⇒ **③ does not hold, ⑥ must not be skipped**. The master's initial skip reason **does not hold**, and after ⑦ pointed this out in round 2 **⑥ was re-run to make up for it** (result in validation report §7.2b). ⇒ Judging "skippable" requires **checking ①②③ item by item**, not inferring from "it looks like documentation".

### 7.2.2 Output contract, cost cap and failure disposition of the ⑥ layer

**⑥ Output contract (Section A–E, isomorphic to ⑦'s contract)**:

- **Section A**: overall verdict line —— `INDEPENDENT SCAN: PASS` or `INDEPENDENT SCAN: FINDINGS` (when transcribed into the `sixstage` fence the block may carry two verdict lines - the first non-blank line after the Section A heading and the block-final line - and both values must be equal; see G7-f in §6.3).
- **Section B**: findings list, each line in the fixed format `SEVERITY | file:line | violated sentence (verbatim quote) | minimal fix`.
- **Section C**: item-by-item evidence and reasoning.
- **Section D**: **falsifiability audit (mandatory)** —— for each item, state "if guard X were deleted, which check/assertion would go red and which would not", and list the invariants it considers **not covered**.
- **Section E**: **"the parts I cannot verify" + "what would overturn my PASS"** (anti-rubber-stamp).

> **Inherited source and sole authority**: the A–E definitions come from v3.1 §2.2 (original text in `docs/t027/FLASH_OPERATING_DIRECTIVE_v3.1.md`). The three references to `Section A–E` in this directive §4.2 / §7.2 / appendix B.6 **take this definition as the sole authority** (completed 2026-09-21: previously those three places referenced it with **no definition**, which was a **reference break**).

**Cost cap**: effective MAI calls for one slice (including first round, resend, re-run after remediation) ≤ **3**; resends are limited to "truncation/anomaly" and count toward the cap; Haiku is counted separately, ≤1.
**Failure handling**: MAI reports Medium+ ⇒ **re-run MAI** after fixing; if MAI's output **deviates in format** (not following Section A–E / no verdict line) ⇒ one resend is allowed; **if it deviates again or the cap is reached ⇒ this layer must not declare a pass**: it must ① truthfully leave a trace and record "⑥ produced no actionable findings"; ② **enter ⑦ independent final review and close the loop per ⑦'s verdict**; ③ escalate to user adjudication when necessary; afterwards ⑨ may still add a "dedicated independent corroboration experiment" to take on the A12 role. **This layer's verdict does not constitute proof of review sufficiency.**

**Mandatory-surface layering (landed v1.33, the ⑥ coverage gap)**: ⑥'s mandatory surface is layered by **evidence availability** - (1) **must check**: the five-section A–E structure, a verdict line in the §4.1 closed set, and agreement between Section B's `SEVERITY` rows and the verdict line; (2) **check when present**: recompute each reading when the input package carries a diff / gate output; (3) **not checked**: a documentation-only slice whose package has no runnable surface carries no run-level assertion. **Basis**: three historical data points (B3c had no Section A–E, v1.30 had no scan verdict line, AT24-WORDING's verdict contradicted its body) ⇒ only format deviations and wasted rounds before this. **Statement**: this layering **does not change** the A–E body.

### 7.2.3 Prerequisites for enabling Haiku

It must be **confirmed before it is enabled**: ① `Claude Haiku 5.5` exists in the Copilot model list; ② the access channel (proxy/network egress) is normal and a first real call completes the availability pre-check. If either is unmet ⇒ **handle it by a three-level fallback**: ① fall back to ⑤ and ask the pre-reviewer for one extra scan round (one extra pre-review round); ② if ⑤ has already converged, then **skip ⑥ and record the reason in the report**, with ⑦ taking over the independent-scan duty; ③ if ⑦ also cannot cover it, then **escalate to user adjudication**; **do not retry repeatedly**, and do not count its absence as a ⑥ failure.

**Call string and internal identifier (added 2026-10-11, closes OB-74)**: Haiku's qualified name = `Claude Haiku 5.5 (copilot)` (the call string, written the same way as the qualified-name column of §2.2, R2.2); internal identifier = `claude-haiku-5.5`. Haiku is an **exemption** outside §2.2 (for the caliber see §2.4), and its internal identifier is **not** a member of the §6.3 G5-b whitelist closed set - that closed set still equals the §2.2 `modelId` (internal identifier) column.

### 7.3 Input sanitization and landing of conclusions

- **Input sanitization**: material handed to the reviewing party must filter out IPs, usernames, hostnames, SSH paths, credentials, and tokens.
- **Verdict landing check**: before landing, every `file:line` given by the reviewing party must be **re-checked item by item** for whether it really exists and matches the criterion; anything that cannot be located is treated as a "lead" and must not be taken at face value.
- **Hard anchoring isolation**: in the first round, ⑦ is given only the slice definition, the diff, the verbatim contract sentences, and read-only constraints; only the **re-review round** may attach a checklist, marked "read only after completing the independent scan".

**Tool-surface calibration (landed v1.33; extended to ⑤/⑥/⑦ in v1.41, landing OB-44 / OB-45)**: the carriers of ⑤/⑥/⑦ have **read-only tools** only (no command execution, no write access) ⇒ (1) **every baseline must be materialized into the review input package**: any reading the reviewer is asked to re-check (gate output, `--selftest` count, diff, file line numbers) must be frozen into static material in the package before dispatch; (2) the reviewer **must not claim to have run a command** - the data source may only read "reading the `<path>` the master wrote to disk" (e.g. "per the measured output inside the input package, …"), and must not write "ran `node` / `gate` or any command"; (3) the reviewer **must not create, modify or delete any file (including under `tmp/`)** - a review report is always returned as the final message and transcribed to disk by the master. ⇒ Isolation is the means; materialization is the only way to satisfy it.

### 7.4 Getting it right the first time (fewer ⑦ rejections)

Before implementation the following are mandatory: ① the plan's change points are precise down to the file; ② the test points are executable and include counter-examples; ③ contract-first has been completed; ④ the gates are fully green locally. **The cost of a rejection is far higher than the cost of upfront clarification.**

### 7.5 Cost caps (hard metrics)

| Role | Per-slice cap | Remarks |
|---|---|---|
| Architect | ≤ 1 | complex/high-risk slices |
| Pre-reviewer | ≤ 1 (re-review after remediation +1) | excludes the ⑦ blind-review add-on |
| ⑥ MAI | ≤ 3 (effective calls) | see §7.2 |
| ⑦ (per §5.2 tier table) · S tier / documentation | 1 (final review) + 1 (re-review) | re-review after remediation is an obligation; the "mandatory-sampled blind review" is counted separately as +1 and may be merged with the latter but **must be judged and its reason recorded before start-up**; **exceptions and stop decisions are in §7.8** |
| ⑦ (per §5.2 tier table) · M tier | 1 (final review) + 2 (re-reviews) | from v1.35: the second re-review must **narrow its input** (repair diff + prior findings + mechanical readings only) and must not re-review the whole text |
| ⑦ (per §5.2 tier table) · L tier / governance / high risk | 1 (final review) + 3 (re-reviews) | from v1.35: the third re-review **checks only the closure path of the previous round's Medium+**; the tier is set once before start-up and **may not be raised mid-slice** |
| Blind review (§7.7 mandatory and proportional sampling) | counted separately as +1 | merging it with any re-review requires a pre-start ruling with a recorded reason; it does not consume the three rows above |

Exceeding a cap ⇒ escalate to user adjudication.

**SELFTEST time budget (added 2026-10-24)**: the per-slice caps of this table count **call counts** only, never elapsed time; the SELFTEST and gate batch runs of ⑨ are planned under the **time-budget caliber** (see the "⑨ native validation and the SELFTEST time budget" paragraph of §5.1) - the budget is a scheduling expectation and **not a wall-clock assertion**; overshooting is neither a failure nor an escalation, only a registered "over budget" with its cause.

**Basis for the quota tiers (v1.35, grounded in ADR-025)**: the former "1 final review + 1 re-review" was **systematically mismatched** with the review volume of governance and L-tier slices - ADR-025 lists seven anchored instances of the "⑦ extra round" class, and its newest instance, `GATES-TIGHTEN`'s ⑦ r1-r3, shows that criterion revisions often need more than one re-review. Three anti-abuse constraints remain after tiering: (1) re-reviews two and three must **narrow their input** and must not re-review the whole text; (2) the tier is **set once before start-up** and may not be raised mid-slice (raising it needs a same-turn user ruling and a separate count); (3) exceeding the tier cap still **escalates to the user** per the table-following sentence of this section. Metering still lists items against R7.1's single source of truth.

**Tier-decision rule (v1.35)**: "tier" in this table means the **§1.2 size tier** and is **not** the §8.2 thinking-mode effort setting nor the §5.2 three-model ⑦ split; when several rows match, take the row with the **most re-reviews**; `M-L` counts as M; a "governance slice" is one that amends this directive or the `tools/gates/**` criteria; a "documentation" slice is one meeting the three conditions of §7.2; a slice already **in flight** when this table takes effect keeps the quota stated in its kickoff until it closes. **Cost-gradient note** (measured pricing, USD per million tokens, offPeak input/output): Flash `0.15/0.6`, V4 Pro `0.66/1.98` ⇒ **prefer pushing coding/testing/documentation/gates down to Flash**.

**⑦ cost definition (v1.43, OB-68 / OB-84 hand-off)**: the unit of this paragraph is Credits, which differs from the USD caliber of the "cost-gradient note" in the same section; the two must not be converted into or merged with each other. The single-read cost of ⑦ = **Input price × input tokens + Output price × output tokens** (both per **1M tokens**, itemized in two columns and never merged into one price); the ⑦-specific pricing is the table below (Credits / 1M tokens, read from the user's operator UI on 2026-10-22):

| ⑦ model (§5.2 tier table) | Input (Credits / 1M tokens) | Output (Credits / 1M tokens) |
|---|---|---|
| `gpt-5.3-codex` | 175 | 1400 |
| `gpt-6.1-sol` | 200 | 1000 |
| `gpt-6-luna` | 20 | 75 |

**Fallback**: if a model's pricing page is unavailable (not displayed), stop and escalate per §1.7.2; **must not estimate or fill in a price on its own**. **Up-front budget caliber**: the estimated per-reading cost = the Input / Output prices in the table × the estimated token volume; the only admissible sources for the estimated token volume are "the median token count of this tier's historical readings" and "the token volume of this slice's declared review input pack"; if neither exists, stop and escalate per §1.7.2 instead of guessing.

**Hard rule R7.1 (source of truth for metering and de-duplication)**: the counts in the table above may only come from a **single source of truth** —— the **session call receipts/logs** (not "I guess I called it a few times"). Every call must be identifiable as `{role, model, stage, startedAt, reason, retryOf}`; **de-duplication rules**: a **resend** of the same `stage` (truncation/anomaly) **counts toward the cap**; a **re-review after remediation** is counted on its own line; **blind review** and final review are listed separately. The report's "cost and metering" must list items by these fields; verbal counts from outside the session logs **do not count**.

### 7.6 Pre-authorized probe quota

Slices that need real external calls must declare a quota cap in advance; when the quota is exhausted, stop, and **must not** add more within the slice. Probe usage must be accounted for (count, number of paid calls, conclusion).

### 7.7 Residual governance (A11 / A12)

- **A11 symmetric spot-check**: the master **randomly samples 1–2 items from each of ⑥'s and ⑦'s "delete guard X ⇒ test Y goes red" claims and actually executes them**; if a claim does not match reality ⇒ that audit's credibility is downgraded and logged as a negative signal. A spot-check only provides **existence corroboration** and does not constitute proof of coverage.
- **A12 independent corroboration**: when ⑥ reports PASS, ⑨ must have at least one **dedicated experiment** that independently proves the key invariant (a criterion unrelated to ⑥).
- **Mandatory-sampled blind review**: when a slice touches **ownership / shutdown / identity** semantics, **1 blind review** is added before ⑦ (with no checklist attached). Its call count and the convention for "whether it is merged with the §7.2 fallback" are **uniformly specified by §7.5**; this section does not accumulate separately, avoiding double counting in two places.
    - **Proportional spot-check (inheriting v3.1 §3.11)**: besides the "mandatory sampling", **1 slice out of every 4** undergoes one ⑦ blind review (choose the one with the broadest reach among the most recent 4 slices); the sampling record (slice number / number of blind-review findings / whether ⑥'s PASS was overturned) is written into the report.
        - **Window counting anchor and record location (OB-82 disposition, 2026-10-16)**: this item is an **additive completion** - the judgement rule and wording of the bullet above stay **verbatim unchanged** ("1 slice out of every 4 (choose the one with the broadest reach among the most recent 4)"); **the window starts at v1.32**, with the counting anchor = the registration order of the "later batch slices" section of the ledger (only closed formal slices count); the "sampled" slice ids and the sampling record are registered slice by slice in the "proportional spot-check record" block at the end of §12.4 (ledger registration; the report side still records per the bullet above); from the first slice of the v1.32 window on, each slice must decide before start and record the reason in its receipt, and a row is registered even when no spot-check occurs, to keep the anchor from drifting.

### 7.8 Review Saturation Criterion (when reviewing may stop)

> **Positioning**: this section is the **sole authority** for the three stop objects (third-party independent re-review / ⑦ round N / re-review after remediation); §5.3 / §7.5 / §12.1 / §12.2 / §12.4 only cite it and never define it separately (one authority, the same hard constraint as Appendix B.7).

**7.8.1 Stop objects and the unit of judgement**: three objects - a **third-party independent re-review** (the DIRECTIVE REVIEW round of a directive-governance slice), **⑦ round N** (the re-review chain after the final review), and a **re-review after remediation** (re-judging a fix diff). The **unit of judgement** = one slice × one review loop; **stopping** = that object is not re-run this round and its conclusion must be labelled truthfully (**not run ≠ PASS**; §7.2's reading "that layer must not declare a pass" is unchanged). ⑥'s cap of three rounds already has a failure disposition in §7.2 and is only cited here.

**7.8.2 Convergence signals (judgement input; each with its test)**:

- **Severity convergence**: no Medium+ this round (§4.3) - mechanically checkable; the verdict line ∈ the closed-set enum of §4.1 is covered by the §4.1 contract and human verification (not G7-c); G7-c checks only the disposition column of finding rows.
- **Finding-rate convergence**: this round's new findings are fewer than the previous round's (per item, same root cause merged, denominator reading in §12.1) - declared by ⑦ in the re-review conclusion.
- **Repetition-rate convergence**: most new findings are recurrences of a registered OB ⇒ recorded as "recurrence" rather than new findings - declared by ⑦.
- **Coverage-gap convergence**: the "parts I cannot verify" of Section E are covered by a mechanical criterion or a registered OB - declared by ⑦.
- **Skip-face substitute-evidence convergence**: every skipped review face's substitute evidence has been declared and argued face by face per the three-tier reading of §7.2.1 - tier (i) must carry the "which aspects covered / which aspects missed" structured statement, and a tier (iii) substitute must have been accepted by ⑦ / the user - declared by ⑦.
- **Quota and exceptions**: see the decision table in 7.8.3.

**7.8.3 Stop decision table**: the four stop situations and the pilot deferral are all decided by the table below.

| Situation | Stop objects it applies to | May stop | Basis section | Record-keeping | May serve as a precedent |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Normal convergence (no Medium+ this round, legal verdict line) | re-review after remediation / ⑦ round N / third-party independent re-review | **May stop** (that object closes) | §4.3 + §5.3 | verdict line verbatim into the report; the re-review report into the review input package (G7 fence block) | yes (the normal flow) |
| Quota exhausted (the §7.5 caps; ⑥'s three rounds in §7.2) | ⑦ round N / third-party independent re-review | **May stop** (no self-funded extra round) | §7.5 + §1.7.2 + §7.2 | unclosed findings registered in §12.4 (`待办：并入下次 ⑦ 输入`); R7.1 metered line by line | no (counted per slice) |
| User-authorized exception (pre-authorized extra round / narrowed input) | ⑦ round N | **May continue** (only within the authorized scope and rounds) | the sentence after the table in §7.5 ("Exceeding a cap ⇒ escalate to user adjudication") + the authorization text | the authorization text + the input-narrowing statement into §12.4 / Appendix C | this slice only (`GATES-EXT` writes explicitly "the authorization does not generalize") |
| OB-11 exception (the ledger-metadata class with all five elements) | re-review after remediation | **May stop** (folded into the next ⑦ input) | §7.8.4 (sole authority) + OB-11 in §12.4 | the OB disposition cell is marked `待办：并入下次 ⑦ 输入` | may serve as a precedent (the same nature only) |
| OB-37 meta-rule (a direct user ruling breaks the literal OB-11 standard) | re-review after remediation | **May stop** (direct user ruling) | §7.8.4 + OB-37 in §12.4 | an independent OB or ADR entry (who ruled / date / reason / basis) + a truthful label | **must not serve as a precedent** |
| Pilot deferral (the two companions in §12.1 + the second pre-ruling in OB-40) | the pilot evaluation (not one of the three review objects) | **Deferral** (not a stop); a second deferral ⇒ escalate for a user ruling | §12.1 + §12.2 + §7.8.6 | "pilot deferred" + the deferral count in the ledger | the second one is a direct user ruling (OB-40); from the third on it must not continue as a precedent |

**7.8.4 Exceptions and meta-rules (sole authority)**:

- **OB-11 exception standard (five elements, restated here as the sole authoritative text)**: a ledger-metadata-class remediation must satisfy all of - **no rule entity**, **no gate impact**, **closure literally verifiable**, **no new semantics**, **ledger-metadata class**; OB-11's disposition cell in §12.4 points here.
